sada to aai thi lekin koi duhai na thi
kaun batae kaun sujhae kaun se des sidhar gae
dil kuchh is surat se tarpa un ko pyaar aa hi gaya
vo khelne ko barf ke gale to de gaya
tamam umr main nakamiyon se kaam liya
sab dil ki shararat hai ankhon ka bahana hai
itni raushan hai tiri subh ki hota hai guman
kabhi jo tujh se mulaqat ho gai pyare
ham aah bhi karte hain to ho jaate hain badnam
dekhiye abr ki tarah ab ke
khuub ham jante hain apni qadr
kya kaha phir to kaho ham nahin sunte teri
agarche zor havaon ne raal rakkha hai
hamare dil men ab talkhi nahin hai
na aae raah pe vo ijz be shumar kiya
ham ko chheroge to pachhtaoge
rang kuchh teri alapon men naya paate hain ham
us ki zulf arasta pairasta
dil ka jaana aaj kal taaza hua ho to kahun
jafa ho ya vafa ham sab men khush hain
main ki vo khaak jise khud hi bikhar jaana tha
mange tange ki qabaen der tak rahti nahin
puchhe lekin puchhne vaale kis ne ye bimar kiya
phir raushan kar zahr ka pyala chamka nai saliben
dam zara lo ki mira dam tum par
achanak pyaar tujh par aa gaya hai
shabab o husn men bahas aa pari hai
rar ham ko bhi lagta hai raste ke sannate se
ab kahan mujh men vo junun sahab
jagmagati hui sarkon pe akele na phiro
dekhte hain ki pahunchti hai vahan kaun si raah
falak deta hai jin ko aish un ko gham bhi hote hain
zafar ik baat par daaim vo hove kis tarah qaaim
kya ajab hai vo mile bhi hon faraz
nashad ham hue to bahut shaad ham hue
maut kuchh tum to nahin ho ki bula bhi na sakun
bulbulen sun kar mire naale ghazal khvan ho gaiin
yaan tak mite ki aap ham apni qasam hue
kaai si jam gai hai ankhon par
agar sharar hai to bharke jo phuul hai to khile
apni hi laash ka khud mazar aadmi
fana ki kaifiyaten dekh jhilmilane men
shama jalti hai to lazim hai shuaen niklen
but parasti jis se hove haq parasti ai zafar
kya ghazab hai ki is zamane men
ik mauj men aaya hua dariya hai ki tum ho
aur har aadmi se khatra hai
zaruri nahin chashm tar kijiye
jhenpa jhenpa sa aa raha hai chand
kaghaz pe qismaton ko sanvar aae ye nahin
aap ki yaad aati rahi raat bhar
mai hai usi ki naam pe jis ke ubal pare
ek mahfil men kai mahfilen hoti hain sharik
abhi kuchh aur ho insan ka lahu paani
shayad kaso ke dil ko lagi us gali men chot
asman bhar gaya hai chilon se

mah akhtar fashan ki bahr istiqbal ankhon se
ek sitara jaldi jaldi ruub gaya
kabhi jaan sadqe hoti kabhi dil nisar hota
suno tumhare gareban bhi nahin mahfuz
koi shirin sukhan hota koi jadu raqam hota
iman mujhe roke hai jo khinche hai mujhe kufr
ab tak to hain aziiz mire bal o par mujhe
ankhon se ansuon ke marasim purane hain
inhin do char gharon men hai thikana tera
aql ki hai maar kyuun kaisi kahi
rahta hai yahan kaun yahan kaun raha hai
samajh ho jis ki baligh samjhe nazar ho jis ki vasia dekhe
vaae gar mera tira insaf mahshar men na ho
dil ko kis shakl se apne na musaffa rakhun
tu sadaon ke bhanvar men mujhe avaz to de
zindagi ham tire daghon se rahe sharminda
ghazal hai naam us ka shaeri men
itna bhi bud o bash ko saada nahin kiya
giri nahin mire hathon se rhaal vaise hi
logon ko ye shikva hai ki ghar par nahin milta
kis jurm se hai chashm tujhe hasrat qubul
to kya sach much judai mujh se kar li
rashk karegi paki e daman

teri ankhon ne to kuchh aur kaha hai mujh se
hijr ki shab hai to kya sakht giran thahri hai
par bahut kam aap men sidq o safa paate hain ham
meri is aadat se us ko bad guman hona hi tha
tulta hai bashar jis men vo mizan hain ankhen
ochhi nigah raal ke ik samt rakh diya
gar khinche siine se navak ruuh tu qalib se khinch
saanp hote hain astinon men
abhi sin hi kya hai jo bebakiyan hon
lab apne kaata kiya main na kamyab hua
dil us be vafa ko jo dete ho akbar
ruumi hai na shami hai kashi na samarqandi
gham ko rahat faza kiya tu ne
aisi rut men to ghane per bhi jhar jaate hain

ja sarv e gulistan kuun khush alhan suun kahunga
phir banaya khuda ne aadam ko
ab ham bhi aa mile to hue mil ke chaar panch

nahin jante kuchh ki jaana kahan hai
jab aa raha tha to ja raha tha

vo chaal aisi chal gaya ham bujh gae dil jal gaya nikle jala ke apna ghar main aur miri avargi

na koi jitne vaala na koi harne vaala

dost ban kar bhi nahin saath nibhane vaala
ab to ye baten bhi ai dil ho gaiin aai gai
zindagi ka nasib kya kahiye

be nava ho ke be nava na bane

fasle nafraton ke mit jaaen

ajnabi dost hamen dekh ki ham
jahan lahu ke samundar ki had thaharti hai
jo fasl khvab ki tayyar hai to ye jaano
zakhm kahte hain dil ka gahna hai
ik aag si vo aur laga kar chale gae
vo aag barsi hai dopahar men ki saare manzar jhulas gae hain
isi liye to kisi ki nazar men aa na sake
sajaya ham ne mazamin ke taaza phulon se

ek ashob vaan se uthta hai

faiz suun tujh firaq ke sajan

bhula diin ek muddat ki jafaen us ne ye kah kar

yahi vo the jinhen hans hans ke jaan dena tha
tum ko bhi to andaza lagana nahin aata

koi dil soz ho to kiije bayan
baithne kaun de hai phir us ko

un ke ghar dagh ja ke dekh liya

hote hote chashm se aaj ashk bari rah gai
be hayai nahin to phir kya hai

ai la ilaah ke varis baaqi nahin hai tujh men

tha dil jab us pe maail tha shauq sakht mushkil
ab to ham ghar se nikalte hain to rakh dete hain

sitam pe khush kabhi lutf o karam se ranjida

mauzun vahin vo khuub hai jo sunte jahan ki hai
koshish bhi kar umiid bhi rakh rasta bhi chun
apne hudud se na barhe koi ishq men

ab ehtiyat ki koi surat nahin rahi
maah ke siine upar ai shama ru
ye kis ka tasavvur hai ye kis ka fasana hai
pur ghurur o pur takabbur pur jafa o pur sitam
farq in men koi itna ziyada to nahin tha
jise gham samajh rahe ho ye agar sharar hota
mere kohsar ke laale hain tahi jam abhi
na gharaz kisi se na vasta mujhe kaam apne hi kaam se

kya koi jism jal raha hai kahin
jo khud ko kah rahe hain ki manzil shanas hain
tum to ho jaan ik zamane ki
khuda ke paak bandon ko hukumat men ghulami men
main vo rone vaala jahan se chala huun
zindagi bhar mere kaam aae usuul
hamare zaiche men zindagi nikalti hai
qasid ki guftugu se tasalli ho kis tarah

ham duur hi se un ko pukar aae ye nahin

kabhi ye marhala jaise ki ashnai na thi
vo ik nigah ki ba zahir nigah se kam hai

kabhi bura nahin jaana kisi ko apne siva

tanha ho to ghabraya sa lagta hai

ghair ko tujh se mohabbat hi sahi

allah ki raah ab tak hai khuli asar o nishan sab qaaem hain

ek dam thi numud buud apni

ab jo palat ke dekhiye baat thi kuchh muhal bhi

kya hai jo badal gai hai duniya
dil men mahak rahe hain kisi aarzu ke phuul
mir ke manind aksar ziist karta tha faraz

koson us ki or gae par sajda har har gaam kiya
hai tira dost magar aadmi achchha bhi nahin
be gunahi hai miri aur saza us ki hai
sirf din rhalne pe mauquf nahin hai mohsin
aah o fughan rafiq hain ye dil ke chaar panch
us ki taqdir men huzur nahin
vah ri ghaflat tujhe apna samajh baithe the ham

thi mere dil ki jo raani amrohe ki raani thi
utthen ki na utthen ye raza un ki hai jalib
basti men aaiye kabhi virana chhor kar

ajab nahin hai agar yaad bhi na aauun use
ab tak to dil ka dil se taaruf na ho saka

un ke dekhe se jo aa jaati hai munh par raunaq
jaati hai jis ki jaan use jaan jaiye

yaad aai tiri javani bhi
ik buzurg aate hain masjid men khizar ki surat
taqdir shikan quvvat baaqi hai abhi is men

rang laaya hai ye lakhe ka jamana tera
khushiyon ke batvare tak hi unche niche aage pichhe
koi badal chhupa tha tishnagi men
ye bhog bhi ek tapasya hai tum tyaag ke maare kya jaano
kahaniyan hi sahi sab mubalghe hi sahi

vo gul huun juda sab se hai jis ka rang

koi guzri hui manzil koi bhuli hui dost
de ke khat munh dekhta hai nama bar

na shab ko din se shikayat na din ko shab se hai
ye tiir dil men magar be sabab nahin utra
kitni khvabida tamannaon ko

aaina dekh kar tasalli hui

hamari taraf ab vo kam dekhte hain
mujh se mil kar udaas bhi ho kya
ham jo ab aadmi hain pahle kabhi

tere visal ke liye apne kamal ke liye

ai shauq vahi mai pi ai hosh zara so ja

ankhon ke tale phirti hai tasvir kisi ki

vo jo divangi ki thi hai abhi

sun ke mira fasana unhen lutf aa gaya
main mutmain huun mira dil tiri panah men hai
yaad karte hain kare yuun hi hamen bhi yaad tu
dastaras men hain anasir ke irade kis ke
go nama bar se khush na hua par hazar shukr
mumkina faislon men ek hijr ka faisla bhi tha
hain fasahat men masal vaaiz o hali donon
ginti ke chaar harfon ka jo naam rah gaya
shaan dekhi nahin gar tu ne chaman men us ki
kisi pe kuchh na zahir ho to koi mehrban kyuun ho
donon ko maarke men gale se mila diya
bada noshi hai bada paimai
raaz hone de ayaan kya hoga
dete nahin kuchh dil ko tasalli ye dilase
dekhe hain ham ne hausle parvardigar ke
shama har rang men jalti hai sahar hote tak
pahuncha jo aap ko to main pahuncha khuda ke taiin
aa gaya ghash nigah dekhte hi
na fikr koi na justuju hai na khvab koi na aarzu hai
vasim kaisi taalluq ki raah thi jis men
ham tiri dosti se rarte hain
apne siine men ise aur zara thaam abhi
aur ham hain ki naam kar rahe hain
khuda ke vaste aise ki phir qasam kya hai
tumhari yaad men jiine ki aarzu hai abhi
ki asman ne tira sheva ikhtiyar kiya
aap hi goya musafir aap hi manzil huun main
us se kya miliye jo har roz kahe kal milna
banaya tor ke aina aina khana
are duniya bari divani hai
aap bahut sharif hain aap ne kya nahin kiya
main nazar bhar ke tire jism ko jab dekhta huun
phir bhi kam kam supurdagi hai abhi
barson men kabhu idhar ham aan nikalte hain
jo shakl nazar aai tasvir nazar aai
zakhm kaise phalte hain daagh kaise jalte hain dard kaise hota hai koi kaise rota hai
makin udhar ke bhi jalve idhar ke dekhte hain
us ne to saare shahr ko kar ke ghulam rakh diya
bharam khul jaae zalim tere qamat ki darazi ka
chale jo zikr farishton ki parsai ka
tu hazar uzr kare magar hamen rashk hai aur hi kuchh jigar
phir kiye se apne ham pachhtaen kya
tiri nigah e pasheman ko kaise dekhunga
nazar men khatke hai bin tere ghar ki abadi
mujhe to duur savera dikhai parta hai
tiri nazar ka dilon se moamla to raha
ruuh pyasi kahan se aati hai
patthar se sar ko phor ki zaanu pe maar haath
haq shanason ke haan khuda hai ishq
shama ru aap go hue lekin
khar o khas to uthen rasta to chale
utho to kya tumhen jaana nahin vatan ki taraf
bas ab to daman e dil chhor do bekar ummido
ham ko yaron ne yaad bhi na rakha
be khudi barhti chali hai raaz ki baten karo
sabza o barg o lala rakhte hain
qatra huun samundar se khafa ho nahin sakta
har koi dil ki hatheli pe hai sahra rakkhe
yuun to qadam qadam pe hai divar samne
ki meri zindagi mujh se khafa hai
bakhsh do gar khata kare koi
hamari baat hi puchhen na vo to kyunkar ho
khizan nasib sahi zehn men bahar bhi rakh
dil kahan ki gum kiije ham ne muddaa paaya
yahi halat ibtida se rahe
koi agar puchhta ye ham se batate ham gar to kya batate
yuun zindagi guzar raha huun tire baghair
dosto dil na lagana na lagana hargiz
aaj daman vasia hai is ka
har naz afrin ko satata raha huun main
zindagi kis tarah basar hogi
jo ho sakta hai us se vo kisi se ho nahin sakta
mujh ko khabar nahin miri mitti kahan ki hai
har ek daur ka mazhab naya khuda laaya
kuchh kami apni vafaon men bhi thi
aankh un se jo milti hai to kya kya nahin hota
thi zor ye kaman vale kham cham bahut hai yaan
tamanna hai ye dil men jab talak hai dam men dam apne
jab bhi ye dil udaas hota hai
haath to kaat diye kuza garon ke ham ne
ki jis se baat us ne shikayat zarur ki
kaha hai kis ne ki ghalib bura nahin lekin
raat ke vaqt mai piye saath raqib ko liye
ye na kah ham se tere munh men khaak
aur dil ka kaha kare koi
juda aghaz se anjam se duur
vo to hai na ashna mashhur aalam men zafar
marhaba main kya mubarak hai giran jani mujhe
kaji jin ki tabiat men hai kab hoti vo sidhi hai
akhbar men jo chahiye vo chhap dijiye
us ne batil se na ta zist kiya samjhauta
daagh chhati ke abas dhota hai kya
lahu charagh jalati rahi hatheli par
nihan bahar thi bulbul tire tarane men
koi thahra ho jo logon ke muqabil to batao
bana hai hamesha ye dil baagh o sahra
us roz se zamin pe sitam asman ke hain
vo na ik pal bhi raha ham se gurezan yaaro
ek dhuen ka marghola sa nikla hai
ghairon ko kab fursat hai dukh dene ki
baat meri kabhi suni hi nahin
naam ko ham bhi yaar rakhte hain
zara sa dard zara sa nishan baaqi hai
tiin sau saal se hain hind ke mai khane band
kabhi kabhi to bara khauf sa lage hai mujhe
zara zulf ko sambhalo mira dil dharak raha hai
hijr ki rut jan leva thi par ghalat sabhi andaze nikle
ik shira khane ki hai divar mere ghar men
meri koi society hi nahin
ab kahan hai bikhar gaya kab ka
yaan tak adu ka paas hai un ko ki bazm men
bikhar kar rah gaya hamsaegi ka khvab hi varna
un paharon men jahan par koi jharna hi nahin
khoi hui shai ki justuju kar
mira shumul har ik dil ke pech o tab men hai
har lams hai jab tapish se aari
jalib apni hi surat the ishq men jaan se jaane vaale
khush huun ki meri baat samajhni muhal hai
surmai ujala hai champai andhera hai
na mile bhiik to lakhon ka guzara hi na ho
ki hairan dekh kar aalam hamen bhi ho tumhen bhi ho
dil ki halat sambhal chali hai
ab maze men ura raha kuchh huun
zulm o sitam se bhi ab zalim ne haath khincha
har bilaval hai des ka maqruz
ham safiron men ham nashinon men
saha to kya na saha aur kiya to kya na kiya
jab insanon ke dil badle to insanon pe kya guzri
had charaghon ki yahan se khatm hai
nazar men phirti hai vo tirgi o tanhai
aae hain mir kafir ho kar khuda ke ghar men
khaki huun magar khaak se rakhta nahin paivand
hijr ke dalan aur angan men bas ik saaya zinda tha
koi suraj mire siine men ubharta hi raha
duniya hai kitni be thikana
jitne men bhi jahan ji ka ziyan pahle se hai
badal ko kya khabar hai ki barish ki chaah men
jaati hai jis pe jaan miri jaan tumhin to ho
ho chuke hali ghazal khvani ke din
vo puchhta tha miri aankh bhigne ka sabab
ham to nakam rahe chahne valon ki tarah
hain javan ikhtiyar rakhte hain
kis ki zulfon men tankti hai abhi
mire dil ne vo naala paida kiya hai
khuun thukun to vah va kiije
hai vasl o hijr apna ai qais turfa mazmun
uthai kyuun na qayamat adu ke kuche men
tum sar ba sar khushi the magar gham mile tumhen
kahin girte girte sambhal gaya kahin chalte chalte thahar gaya
mujhe be dast o pa kar ke bhi khauf us ka nahin jaata
mail ba ghair hona tujh abru ka aib hai
muddaten ho gaiin faraz magar
meri fughan se rustakhez kaaba o somnat men
baat saari zamir hi ki hai
vo ashna jis se muddaton ajnabi raha huun
tha us sukut men bhi maza us gali men chal
dasht men hai mujhe vo aish ki ghar yaad nahin
tamatto ek ka hai ek ke nuqsan se aalam men
dil shikan hi sahi par baat baja us ki hai
is mahina bhar kahan tha saqiya achchhi tarah
dil tira faulad ho to aap ho aina var
jis ke mast o kharab hain ham log
kuchh apne dil ki khu ka bhi shukrana chahiye
ashk khunin suun jo kiya hai vazu
apni tishna labi se khatra hai
divana na samjhe hamen vo samjhe sharabi
juun duud faraham huiin rauzan men nigahen
kaash duniya mire ehsas ko vapas kar de
sada kaha yahi main ne qarib tar hai vo duur
ki bure vaqt men ho jaae thikana dil ka
ja ke aine pe chehre ki ziya ulti phiri
rafaqaton ka miri us ko dhyan kitna tha
husn ke khizr ne kiya labrez
go rashk se jala tire qurban to gaya
ham se nadani hui ya tum se nadani hui
jaan di di hui usi ki thi
kaise kah duun ki mujhe chhor diya hai us ne
ashk afshani bhi meri gauhar afshani hui
dil ko is ahd men ham kaam men la hi na sake
aa gaya tha mire guman men kya
tumhari tarz o ravish jante hain ham kya hai
avval avval ki mohabbat ke nashe yaad to kar
khalvat ki ghari guzri jalvat ki ghari aai
dil se khayal e dost bhulaya na jaega
aag men jo para vo aag hua
mah ne aa samne shab yaad dilaya tha use
hijr ho ya visal ho kuchh ho
vo tund vaqt ki rau hai ki paanv tik na saken
tumhare payami ne sab raaz khola
lagte hi us ke haath ki talvar mar gae
sab rang men us gul ki mire shaan hai maujud
itna miliye khaak men jo khaak men rhunre koi
ki tu zinda to hai ab tak magar kyuun
maut se bhi khatm jis ka silsila hota nahin
hai bahut dushvar marna ye suna karte the ham
magar falak ko adavat usi ke ghar se thi
ki apni mauj se begana rah sakta nahin dariya
is musibat men dil se kya kahta
jab asman se koi faisla utarta hai
barbadiyon ka jashn manata chala gaya
tumhare phuul aur shabnam hamari
dil bhi pagal hai ki us shakhs se vabasta hai
sansen jitni maujen utni sab ki apni apni ginti
ham par kitni baar pare ye daure bhi tanhai ke
ghar se chala to dil ke siva paas kuchh na tha
ye zindagi hai to phir zindagi ko kya kiije
le ke dil dil sitan ravana hua
umr bhar intizar kar baithe
ki dil se be qarari ja rahi hai
ki dil men kyuun tira gham pal raha hai
kya haqiqat hai tujh tavazo ki
yaani ghafil ham chale sota hai kya
pyaar ki baat na puchho yaaro
falak se koi utarta dikhai parta hai
vo nazuk badan paanv bhari lage
diin de ke gaya kufr ke bhi kaam se ashiq
main phuul chunti rahi aur mujhe khabar na hui
abhi to chaak mire zakhm ke sile bhi nahin
kisi ko is ki khabar nahin hai mariz ka dam nikal raha hai
nikal aate hain aansu hanste hanste
khusrav o kaiqibad ki tegh nahin kamar nahin
ye teri tavajjoh ka hai ejaaz ki mujh se
tum apni baanh na dena hava ki banhon men
yahi hai azmana to satana kis ko kahte hain
ki gir pare na mire paanv par dar o divar
vo yahan se chala gaya hai kahin
ehsan liye kitne masiha nafason ke
ishq men yadgar ham bhi hain
marhale ab dekhiye pesh aaen kya
na ho jab dil hi siine men to phir munh men zaban kyuun ho
jo zindagani ko talkh kar de vo vaqt mujh par guzar chuka hai
gar kiya naseh ne ham ko qaid achchha yuun sahi
ye kis bahisht shamail ki aamad aamad hai
mile hain ham ko vo rahbar khuda ke raste men
kuchh ghaur se qatil ka hunar dekh rahe hain
ik umr sher se raha bezar mushafi
zahir hua ki daagh ka sarmaya duud tha
zinhar na hona taraf in be adabon se
jo diya ai khuda diya tu ne
chhata jahan se us avaz ka ghana badal
parsa khush tan o nau khez javan
is takalluf se ki goya but kade ka dar khula
saara manzar hara sa rahta hai
tu jahan naaz se qadam rakh de
bin gul mua hi main to pa tu ja ke lautiyo
dabi dabi tire lab se hansi nikalti hai
ibtida ko jo intiha jaane
diida ta dil hai yak aina charaghan kis ne
us be haya ki charb zabani kuun dekh tuun
gul ke charagh gul ho yak baar jhar paren sab
kanch ke pyalon men sandal bhigta hai saath saath
kabhi lahu se bhi tarikh likhni parti hai
koi fitna barpa hua chahta hai
zafar main apni haqiqat kahun to kis se kahun
samajhta huun ki rhunre hai abhi se barq khirman ko
jaise chhanti ho sitaron ki kiran kya kahna
sabr tuute hue dilon ka na le
kahin aise bigre sanvarte bhi dekhe
akhiri baar ik ghazal sun lo
ham ne kya us se mohabbat ki ijazat li thi
khuub ummiden bandhin lekin huiin hirman nasib
mohabbat men firaq itna na gham kar
apne bhi khafa mujh se hain begane bhi na khush
ja ke kohsar se sar maaro ki avaz to ho
be sutun samne se apne uthaya na gaya
us se utthegi kya musibat e ishq
kabhi kabhi main ye sochta huun ki mujh ko teri talash kyuun hai
ek nadi men kitne bhanvar
bandh lo haath ki phailen na kisi ke aage
jin ki nagri hai vo janen ham thahre banjare log
jo hausle the vo dil hi dil men rahe daregh o darang ho kar
ye tere vasl ka qissa to ik bahana hua
jahannam ho ki jannat jo bhi hoga faisla hoga
na tu zamin ke liye hai na asman ke liye
phuul hona hi nahin phuul nazar aana bhi
ta chand rahegi ye shab e gham ki siyahi
zamin ki khair mangen asman se
sukhan huun sukhan bar lab avurdagan ka
ham vahan hain jahan se ham ko bhi
lahu chashida haath us ne chuum kar dikha diya
mere khilaf zahr ugalta phire koi
hunar paida kiya hai main ne hairat azmai men
paas rah kar bhi hamesha vo bahut duur mila
marne ki ai dil aur hi tadbir kar ki main
marta huun is avaz pe har chand sar ur jaae
tum jalte ho kyuun jiita huun kyuun gaata huun
koi aisi misal thi hi nahin
ham ne ik baat aaj jaani hai
be hijabi se tiri tuuta nigahon ka tilism
main ne majnun pe larakpan men asad
us fasl men ki gul ka gareban bhi hai hava
tuut jaaen ki pighal jaaen mire kachche ghare
vaae mahrumi khazaf chain lab sahil huun main
aksar miri qaba pe hansi aa gai jise
chuur tha zakhmon men aur kahta tha dil
is tavaqqo pe ki khud se bhi juda ho jaun
bina pairon ke sar chalte nahin hain
tumhen to dil lagi hoti gharibon par sitam hota
yuun jo takta hai asman ko tu
vasim dekhna mur mur ke vo usi ki taraf
sabza o gul kahan se aae hain
ab tak ke tire saath pe hairat bhi bahut thi
anis dam ka bharosa nahin thahar jaao
phir kyuun na raha ghar ka vo naqsha koi din aur
ham ko gar tu ne rulaya to rulaya ai charkh

jahan saaqi ho tu batil hai daava hoshiyari ka
is zindagi men itni faraghat kise nasib
chalne ka hausla nahin rukna muhal kar diya
naam apna saba saba kiije
uruuj teri khabar jab zaval leta hai
qalam se harf jo nikle sharar hi yak qalam nikle
agle vaqton ke hain ye log inhen kuchh na kaho
jo zamane ko bura kahte hain khud hain vo bure
vaqf hai yaan barahna paanv par
aaj shamshan ki si bu hai yahan
koi mauja e shirin chuum kar jagaegi
na nikle shama ke pa se nikale gar na khaar atish
abhi talak to na kundan hue na rakh hue
jaane kya intizam kar rahe hain
us se bhi aaj liije mulaqat ho gai
qurbaton men bhi judai ke zamane mange
tajrabe suun hua mujhe zahir
til nazik us ke jiyun sanasi hai
shelf men rakkhi hui band kitabon ki tarah
ham se to baat baat pe jhagra kiya na jaae
salamat malamat malamat salamat
zid par ishq agar aa jaae
main ne bas haath men sipar li hai
paat hare hain phuul khile hain kam kam bad o baran hai
kaaba mire pichhe hai kalisa mire aage
bhala use na sahi kuchh mujhi ko rahm aata
baat bigri bani banai aaj
kisi ko kyuun kahen ham bad ki bad gui se kya hasil

abhi jalta ho koi pal shayad

hua khaak ab ye hai anjam dil ka

aise lipte hain dar o bam se ab ke jaise
kahne ki baat hai ki jiye ja raha huun main

logon ko jab aapas men larana nahin aata
koi to aisa ghar hota jahan se pyaar mil jaata
vaqt pahle guzar gaya hoga
aql men yuun to nahin koi kami

dushman ko to pahlu se vo talne nahin dete

aaj udhar hi ko rahega dida e akhtar khula
ye rasm barham fana hai ai dil gunah hai jumbish nazar bhi

jis qadar main ne tujh se khvahish ki

magar ashufta bayani meri
kal isi mitti se shayad miri shakhen niklen

us shama ki majlis men jaana hamen phir vaan se

shaikh ki davat men mai ka kaam kya

us ne jab hans ke namaskar kiya

zof se ai girya kuchh baaqi mire tan men nahin
dar haqiqat jaise mujh ko etibar aa hi gaya
ham ko aur to kuchh nahin sujha albatta us ke dil men
nazar men dhuul jigar men liye ghubar chale
ishq mujh ko nahin vahshat hi sahi
be sabab dhum dham kar rahe hain
jab se dekha hai tujhe mujh se hai meri an ban
ye mat likkho vo mat likkho aae bare samjhane vaale
har kisi ko salam kar rahe hain
vo ek din ek ajnabi ko
lakhon men bas vahi allah ka pyara nikla
ai miri gul zamin tujhe chaah thi ik kitab ki
haq to yuun hai ki haq ada na hua
zindagi ab to ik tamanna de
kaun hoga jo mujhe us ki tarah yaad kare
faza khanda gul tang o zauq aish be parda
lo qayamat ab aai vo kafir
khud ko ganva ke kaun tiri justuju kare
public men zara haath mila lijiye mujh se
ai muhavvis jab ki zar tere nasibon men nahin
ye kaam kis ne kiya hai ye kaam kis ka tha
aaj socha to aansu bhar aae
shokh ho jaati hai ab bhi tiri ankhon ki chamak
jo gul ruyan men paaya etibar ahista ahista
zulfen siina naaf kamar
surat pazir ham bin hargiz nahin ve maane
ham ne jab khaai hai apne hi se zak khaai hai
be sahara tha bahut pyaar koi puchhta kya
bandhi zahid ne tavakkul par kamar sau baar chust
ham se vaada tha ik savere ka
dekho ye shahr hai ajab dil bhi nahin hai kam ghazab
ik zara divangi darkar hai
na puchh mujh se ki shahr valon ka haal kya tha
phir vahi khair nigahi hai tire jalvon se
nae charagh jala raat ho gai pyare
phir vo larki mujhe mili hi nahin
ibarat mukhtasar qasid bhi ghabra jaae hai mujh se
shahr khali kiye dukan kaisi
main ne bhi un ki vo khabar li hai
nashe men chuur na hon jhanjh men makhmur na hon
us ki banki ada ne jab maara
kaha jo us ne zara mere paanv daab to de
milti hai paas aane ki mohlat kabhi kabhi
jab paani men chehra dekha
jaate kahan ho aina o shana chhor kar
ki ye kahe ki sar e rahguzar hai kya kahiye
main ek qatra huun tanha to bah nahin sakta
udasiyon ki koi shahriyat nahin hoti
dukh hua karta hai kuchh aur bayan
vaqt ne kis shabih ko khvab o khayal kar diya
koi qatil se milta hai koi bismil se milta hai
uth kar to aa gae hain tiri bazm se magar
aina khana men koi liye jaata hai mujhe
samjhe vo apna khaksar mujhe
is men hai ik baat honour ki shifa ho ya na ho
vo gulsitan ki jahan ghaat men na ho sayyad
kya janiye jigar ko ya dil ko rhunrte hain
ek tera dar hi kya ham to zamana chhor den
khubi men gul rukhan suun mumtaz hai sarapa
apne ishq men sachcha chand

apne sar ik bala to leni thi
chalte chalte ruk jaata huun saari ki dukanon par
shifa apni taqdir hi men na thi
patthar se visal mangti huun
mujh ko tamam hosh bana kar chale gae
muaaf kar na saki meri zindagi mujh ko
ye muqaddar ka sitam bhulne vaala hai kahin
kaash is khvab ki taabir ki mohlat na mile
ye paap hai kya ye pun hai kya riton par dharm ki mohren hain
gham to ye hai ki tum bhi bahut kam mile tumhen
vahin main khaak urti dekhta huun
vali kuun mat malamat kar ai vaaiz
chhuta qafas se to parvaz ki chaman ki taraf

roya huun to apne doston men
koi to hai jo havaon ke par katarta hai
kabhi to chaunk ke dekhe koi hamari taraf
vahin pe ghumte rahna to koi baat nahin
jis zakhm ko chirun huun paikan nikalte hain
koi to apne ghar gaya hoga
is se barh kar miri tahsin bhala kya hogi

kamre men sajane ko musibat nahin milti
vo gae to aafat laae hai har taal men leve jaan nikal
kar liya saari khudai se bigar
gham nahin likkhun kya main gham ko jashn likhun kya matam ko
jo vo mire na rahe main bhi kab kisi ka raha

apne chehre se jo zahir hai chhupaen kaise

ham rashk ko apne bhi gavara nahin karte
tu hai pahlu men phir tiri khushbu

na to dam leti hai tu aur na hava thamti hai

tere dil ko abhi lagi hi nahin

dekhna in bastiyon ko tum ki viran ho gaiin

ek aalam ke sar bala laaya

to nirasi kahan se aati hai

sust rau jaise koi abla pa hota hai
nazar rahat pe meri kar na vaada shab ke aane ka
nae pahlu nikalte ja rahe hain
vo ye kahte hain bara dil hai tavana tera
duniya ke zulm sahte rahe khamushi se ham
ai vali gul badan kuun baagh men dekh
dard dil ka libas hota hai

raat hamen kuchh yaad nahin tha
chand lamhe jo laut kar aae
na shoale men ye karishma na barq men ye ada

rangin fitrat saada tabiat
yuun bichharna bhi bahut asan na tha us se magar
be nam sa ye dard thahar kyuun nahin jaata
ek mere hi siva sab ko pukare hai koi
ghair yuun karta hai meri pursish us ke hijr men
ne asad jafa sail ne sitam junun mail

vo aae aa ke chale bhi gae mile bhi nahin

niind ka kitna kachcha chand

lekin in pyasi ankhon se ab tak aansu bahte hain
par kya karen ki ab ke safar hi ajiib tha
mujhe ghata ke vo ginti men rah nahin sakta
ham bayaban men hain aur ghar men bahar aai hai
umr shayad na kare aaj vafa
va nahin hota kisu se dil girafta ishq ka
ishq ka qarz chukaya hai bahut din ham ne
miri maut ko bhi pyare koi chahiye bahana
mahfil men aa gae hain vo apne nasib se
husn ke jaane kitne chehre husn ke jaane kitne naam
khote damon men ye bhi kya thahra
kahan nasib ki chumen malak jabinon ko
apni nazar se duur to mujh ko jaane de
jo tufanon men palte ja rahe hain
kisi ko kya ho dilon ki shikastagi ki khabar
maan liije shaikh jo daava kare
gar is qaid e bala se ab ki chhutenge to phir hargiz
aur halqe hain sab amiron ke
tum shaad zindagani karo gham bahut hai yaan
kya rahun ghurbat men khush jab ho havadis ka ye haal
hai ajab kuchh moamla darpesh
ik tiri diid chhin gai mujh se
be niyazi ki shaan kahti hai
zad se ulfat ki bach ke chalna tha
aaj kuchh dard mire dil men siva hota hai
ana parast hai itna ki baat se pahle
raat mujrim thi daman bacha le gai
zamana ghaur se sunta hua lage hai mujhe
koi vaada koi iqrar masihai ka
ne tiir kaman men hai na sayyad kamin men
aa ki miri jaan ko qarar nahin hai
munh subh dikha jaana phir shaam chhupa jaana
marta huun main ki ye na kisi ki nigah ho
sab gale milne lage jab ki vo jallad aaya
main aur jaun dar se tire bin sada kiye
mujh pe chha jaae vo barsat ki khushbu ki tarah
bahut duur ab hai dil se yaad teri
donon insan hain to kyuun itne hijabon men milen
nadan jise kahte hain taqdir ka zindani
sirf surat ka band naiin naji
kahin nicha na ho ai gor sirhana tera
kaha mar jaae chupke se to phir mujh ko khabar kya ho
so is fan ko aisa bara kar chale
na karta kaash naala mujh ko kya malum tha hamdam

aana jaana nahin ki tujh se kahen
koi mila to haath milaya kahin gae to baten kiin
apne pe etimad hai ghair ko azmae kyuun
justuju aaj bhi usi ki hai
fulan ke zakhm achchhe the fulan se
bhala ghamon se kahan haar jaane vaale the
ji ke ziyan ko ishq men us ke apna vaara jaane hai
hamari guftugu ki aur bhi samten bahut si hain
kab nikalta hai ab jigar se tiir
ham ko bhi bech de ham bhi usi bazar se hain
charagh bantta phirta hai chhin kar ankhen
pakre jaate hain farishton ke likhe par na haq
ki vo chilman men hain nazdik ham chilman ke baithe hain
main zindagi ka saath nibhata chala gaya
tarash kar mire baazu uraan chhor gaya
kise nasib ki be pairahan use dekhe
ab na vo raat jab ummiden bhi kuchh thiin tujh se
taras rahe hain javan phuul hont chhune ko
khuda ke vaste zahid utha parda na kaabe ka
aur ham ne rote rote dupatte bhigo liye
log vo darmiyan ke the hi nahin
be dast o pa huun aaj to ilzam kis ko duun
nahangon ke nasheman jis se hote hain tah o bala
tum sargiran to mujh se na ho main nashe men huun
maadum ki havas hai zaail ko rhunrte hain
jis tarah sukhe hue phuul kitabon men milen
mere hi ghar men rahta hai mujh jaisa hi jaane kaun
kaam kab phul pan se nikla
tere dar tak pahunch ke laut aae
jab us rim jhim ki vaadi ke afsane yaad aate hain
yaad aa gaya mujhe tiri divar dekh kar
lahu ki garm bundon ko charaghan ham nahin kahte
tariki ke naam likhun to qaumen firqe zaat likhun
dil ajab shahr ki jis par bhi khula dar is ka
yakka taz o neza baz o arbada ju tund khu
vo chup chap aansu bahane ki raten
aalam men log milne ki gon ab nahin rahe
dekhenge sher par dil e divana chhor kar
jin khvabon ko dekh ke main ne jiina sikha
itni muddat baad mile ho
gulsitan ki baat rangin hai na mai khane ka naam
is mukh ko tire safha e quran suun kahunga
chalte phirte raushan raste tariki men ruub gae
zindagi se kisi samjhaute ke ba vasf ab tak

dil suun sab ram ram karte hain
ashar mire yuun to zamane ke liye hain
aaj kya naddi bahi ulti hava ulti phiri

avara mizaji ne phaila diya angan ko

la phir ik baar vahi baada o jaam ai saaqi
dekhiye aaj yaad aae kaun
aankh to kar chaar kyuun kaisi kahi
mujh ko bas teri khushi darkar hai
rumal do do din tak juun abr tar rahe hai
hui muddat ki ghalib mar gaya par yaad aata hai
saari umr kahan thahra hai koi ek rihaish par
vo dikhaya jo tha maqam kharab
bahut dard mandon ko dekha hai tu ne
is rang se jhamke hai palak par ki kahe tu

khul ke milne ka saliqa aap ko aata nahin
tire badalne ke ba vasf tujh ko chaha hai
ai nazm kya bataen hajj o tavaf apna
yuun to na tere jism men hain zinhar haath
us larki ne mujh se bichhar kar mar jaane ki thani thi
bhanvre amrit ras ki dhun men pal pal sau sau baar gae

vo chahe nazm ka tukra ki nasr para ho
aur ab naraz khushbu ke bayan se
ajal mar rahi tu kahan aate aate
kahan har ek se insaniyat ka baar utha
farhad o qais o vamiq o azra the chaar dost
rahman ki rahmat ho ki bhagvan ki murat
magar ye roz gai baat chher deti hai
ab milen bhi to na pahchan saken ham un ko
shaam se aa gae jo piine par
vo ik makan abhi tak makin ki chaah men hai
us shama ki tarah se jis ko koi bujha de
ab bhi barsat ki raton men badan tutta hai
raat bahut hi yaad aae ho
bahar aate aate khizan aate aate
darpai hain mere qatl ke ahbab aur main
aap ab shauq se de len jo saza dete hain
vahin dil ka kanval lahra raha hai
aise men aao to janen aise men kab aate ho
kya jaane kis ki pyaas bujhane kidhar gaiin
qismat khuli tire qad o rukh se zuhur ki
aah ab mujh se tiri ranjish e beja bhi nahin
ham na samjhe ki ye aana hai ki jaana tera
ab kaun hai shirin hai ki laila hai ki tum ho
lazim tha ki dekho mira rasta koi din aur
duniya ko tere dard ne duniya bana diya
pagal to nazar aate hain pagal nahin hote
ki na shab ki kabhi pazirai
laga laga ke gale se chhuri ko pyaar kiya
huur o khiyam se guzar bada o jam se guzar
parva nahin ik maan ka jo dil tuut gaya hai
ghar palat aauun to main abla pa ho jaun
na vo dekhte hain na ham dekhte hain
saaat aa pahunchi us judai ki
ta miri jaan tu kahin khalq men badnam na ho
hasil hai kya sivae tarai ke dahr men
vo aa raha mire ham sae men to saae se
agarche rah men huiin jag hansaiyan kya kya
magar charagh ne lau ko sambhal rakkha hai
bina rakkhi hai gham par ziist ki ye ho gaya sabit
karta hai chhed chhed hamara jigar tamam
dil men ab yuun tire bhule hue gham aate hain
malum ab hua ki bahut main bhi duur tha
kis se puchhun ki kahan gum huun kai barson se
dil andheron men raha us ka farozan yaaro
ashiq tire adam ko gae kis qadar tabah
khuddari o mahrumi mahrumi o khuddari
kahun main jis se use hove sunte hi vahshat
yuun na luti thi galiyon daulat apne ashkon ki

vo nahin malum muj ko naseha kya chiiz hai
jo tere aariz o gesu ke darmiyan guzre

tire mathe tire bistar ki shikan bhi ab ke
kaun si baat kahi itne yaqin se ham ne

har su dikhai dete hain vo jalva gar mujhe
us sa hindu na koi us sa musalman yaaro
divanon ke ashkon ki ghata saath liye ja
hosh jaata nahin raha lekin
kuchh to malal is ka ho dil ko mire magar nahin
chalo is tarah se sajaen use
kam kisu ko mir ki mayyat ki haath aai namaz
ham se durveshon ke ghar aao to yaron ki tarah
jaan tum par nisar kaun kare
har ajnabi hamen mahram dikhai deta hai
chhanv paate hi so gaya jaise
main samundar dekhti huun tum kinara dekhna
sun ke sitam zarif ne mujh ko utha diya ki yuun
jin ko apni khabar nahin ab tak
magar bad nam khilqat ho rahi thi
mere gham khane ki qismat jab raqam hone lagi
ayat men rhal ja kabhi jibril dahan tu
tasavvur huun be mojib azurdagan ka
jaan se jab us men guzre tab hamen rahat hui
pahle kahte the faqat munh se aur ab karna para

hanste hanste kabhi thak jaao to chhup ke ro lo
kya kya na ham kharab hue hain magar ye dil
kuchh aisi baat na thi tera duur ho jaana

hushyar ho ghaflat se tu ghafil na ho ai dil
mera chhappar utha gaya koi
us ki ummat men huun main mere rahen kyuun kaam band
ye madad mujh pe asmani hai
mere aansu ki us ne ki tarif
us shokh ko bhi raah pe laana zarur tha
vagarna taab o tavan bal o par men khaak nahin
main pashiman huun ujala kar ke

ham pe ik etiraz ye bhi hai
phir aaj kis ne sukhan ham se ghaebana kiya
dil kash o khushnuma diya tu ne
dil ko jo dekh lo to yahi pyaar se kaho
ghair ko ashna kiya tu ne
nikal aae koi pahchan zara dekh to lo
sapn salona prem khilauna phuul bichhauna vo pahlu
tu miri pahli mohabbat thi miri akhiri dost
faeda kya soch akhir tu bhi daana hai asad
yaan aa pari ye sharm ki takrar kya karen
agar palak pe hai moti to ye nahin kaafi
hazaron hijabon men parvana dekha
ishq hi ishq hai jahan dekho

vo jo taamir hone vaali thi
bargad ki ik shakh hata kar

ye dil hai ise dil hi bas rahne diije

vo to jab aate hain mail ba karam aate hain
hansne se ho sukun na rone se kal pare
kha gae the vafa ka dhoka ham
aaj gautam ki zamin tulsi ka ban azad hai

ik vaqia sa yaad dila kar chale gae

nahin nigah men manzil to justuju hi sahi

vo sadaqat maab hain ham log
vahi begane chehre hain jahan jaaen jidhar jaaen

kaun si baat hai tum men aisi

kis suun vali apas ka ahval ja kahun main

hai kahan tamanna ka dusra qadam ya rab

kis kaam ki rahi ye dikhave ki zindagi

dashne ne kabhi munh na lagaya ho jigar ko

mehrban na mehrban kya kya samajh baithe the ham
us ke kuche men hain vo be par o bal
ye vo raat thi ki guzar gai ye vo nashsha tha ki utar gaya
qafas se aaj saba be qarar guzri hai
na jaane vaqt ki raftar kya dikhati hai
kahin anjam aa pahuncha vafa ka
daulat hai gham zakat diye ja raha huun main
har uqda men jahan ke poshida hai kashakash

aisi kashti ka rubna achchha

dar o divar pe kuchh saae hain

tum to hamen karvat bhi badalne nahin dete

qata hone hi jab laga paivand
kal dopahar ajiib si ik be dili rahi

khaak hi avval o akhir thahri

vo jo kaghaz men dava bandhte hain

mir o ghalib bane yagana bane

mantiq bhi to ik chiiz hai ai qibla o kaaba
zamana sang ba kaf hai to shishagar ankhen
aaj main ne apna phir sauda kiya
inhin sifat se hota hai aadmi mashhur
chaak hota hai gareban se juda mere baad
itna surur tha ki mujhe bhi surur tha
par ik sukun tha ham ko fareb khane men
sab ko apni hi kisi baat pe rona aaya
bulaya jo ghairon ko davat men tum ne
mit gaya par tiri banhon ne sameta na mujhe
mere haal pe hairat kaisi dard ke tanha mausam men
kabhi hayat kabhi mai haram hoti rahi
tujh zulf ke halqe men hai dil be sar o be pa
na vo ishq men rahin garmiyan na vo husn men rahin shokhiyan
baare duniya men raho gham zada ya shaad raho
uthne vaali hai bazm maazi ki
jaan kar jo na muddaa jaane
tere siva bhi ham pe bahut se sitam hue
kis ko fursat thi ki batlata tujhe itni si baat
ishq ki lau se farishton ke bhi par jalte hain
main jo bola kaha ki ye avaz
main zinda huun ye mushtahar kijiye
naam ka mere hai jo dukh ki kisi ko na mila
jis ko dekho gaya hua hai kahin
dil men tujh se thi jo shikayat
safina jab ki kinare pe aa laga ghalib
kitni der aur main rukun sahab
baagh pa kar khafaqani ye rarata hai mujhe
garche dil khol ke dariya ko bhi sahil bandha
main dagh huun marta huun idhar dekhiye mujh ko
ishq kiije phir samjhiye zindagi kya chiiz hai
ghalib mujhe hai us se ham aghoshi aarzu
tu bhi nadan hua jaata hai
usi jallad ka likha hua hai
usi khana kharab ki si hai
utha chuke hain zamindar jin zaminon ko
jo kiya tha yaad sab tha bhuul jaane ke liye
jalib pukarti hain vo shola navaiyan
khush bu kahin na jaae pa israr hai bahut
ruuh ganga ki himala ka badan azad hai
kaam aisa sikha diya tu ne
be sabab baar baar the ham to
zafar vo zahid e bedard ki hu haq se behtar hai
mire hisse ki goya har khushi kam hoti jaati hai
ab jis taraf se chahe guzar jaae karvan
har ek bare naam se marub rahe hain
us zulf ke khayal se azad ham hue
jaenge na tamam ye afsana chhor kar
mohabbaton men to milna hai ya ujar jaana
jise ishq ki be qarari lage
khushbu ghunche talash karti hai
tere rukhsar ki safai dekh
khush na aaya hamen jiye jaana
pahle haqiqaton hi se matlab tha aur ab
ham to be nam iradon ke musafir hain vasim
khanjar se chiir siina agar dil na ho do nim
aaj hi hua manzur un ko imtihan apna
dil kuun tujh baaj be qarari hai
uryan hain tire chaman ki huren
ye soch le ki main bhi tiri khvahishon men huun
us shokh ne to dekhna akhbar bhi chhora
pukarte rahe mahfuz kashtiyon vaale
rang be rang hon khushbu ka bharosa jaae
aaj phir un ki aamad aamad hai
us par sapand var na itna uchhal ke chal
ik ghar men mukhtasar bayaban zarur tha
miri fariyad maari ja rahi hai
ankhen manzar huiin kaan naghma hue
ai dost mire vaste kuchh aur na ban tu
garm lashen girin fasilon se
main bataun sab ko hoga yaad sab
pahlu tahi na kar gham o andoh se asad
aur bhi us shokh ko nazdik tar paata huun main
karun zikr kya main shabab ka sune kaun qissa ye khvab ka
zinat muqaddama hai musibat ka dahr men
apne dil ko figar kar baithe
barh ke siine se na lipta le mira qatil mujhe
khabron ne ki musavvari khabren ghazal banin
tumhare shahr men kuchh aur kam kaj bhi ho
ab tira naam hai bas ishq ke gham khanon men
le chala jaan miri ruuth ke jaana tera
ye samajh kar tujhe ai maut laga rakkha hai
mujh ko insan se autar kiya
koi atka hua hai pal shayad
aisa kahan se laun ki tujh sa kahen jise
bhule se mohabbat kar baitha nadan tha bechara dil hi to hai
chaman men naaz bulbul ne kiya jo apni naale par
qashqa khincha dair men baitha kab ka tark islam kiya
na thikana hai jigar ka na thikana dil ka
kahti hai vo apne hathon khalq men rusva hain aap
suraj ko chonch men liye murgha khara raha
faqat apna sahara rah gaya hai
dil men kitne khush the apni furqat ki araish par
kya hua aaj ye kis baat pe rona aaya
dard kuchh din to mehman thahre
dil e pur dard se kuchh kaam lunga
jis mathe ki khatir tha ik tiika chand
aage hai ishq jurm o saza ke maqam se
jhar ke utthe apna daman
rizq pahunchata hai ghar baithe khuda mere liye
ham na hote to kisi aur ke charche hote
tapka pare hai rang chaman men hava se aaj
yaan munasib nahin ro ro ke rulana hargiz
miri tarah se koi hai jo zindagi apni
tiri duuri pe vaari ja rahi hai
mar kar bhi haath aave to mir muft hai vo
zinda ho tu to be huzur nahin
thakan se chuur paas aaya tha us ke
hilte hain khud ba khud mire andar kafan ke paanv
kanch ke pichhe chand bhi tha aur kanch ke uupar kaai bhi
subh hi raat utari thi aur shaam ko shab pahnai bhi
to fakhr kya jo hua etibar ham par bhi
ghalib ko bura kyuun kaho achchha mire aage
badan mera chhua tha us ne lekin
na gai teri be rukhi na gai
ik ajab taur haal hai ki jo hai
guhar men mahv hua iztirab dariya ka

aur phir kaun se naale ko rasa kahte hain
zamin hogi kisi qatil ka daman ham na kahte the
asar bhi le raha huun teri chup ka
hijr ke dasht men karvan ek main
jal gaya apna nasheman to koi baat nahin

magar nigah men thora sa intizar bhi rakh
saaf chhupte bhi nahin samne aate bhi nahin
jaane kya kya bol raha tha sarhad pyaar kitaben khuun
per par pak gaya hai phal shayad
vahi halat hain faqiron ke
ek vo din jab per ki shakhen bojh hamara sahti thiin
ki jyuun gulshan men aati hai bahar ahista ahista
asman ka hi ishara aur hai
be niyazi tiri aadat hi sahi
dosti apni bhi kuchh roz zamane se rahi
khvahish ko ahmaqon ne parastish diya qarar
dabe paanv munh pher kar jaane vaale
dida o dil ka bhi aksar vasta hota nahin
us ki mutthi men bahut roz raha mera vajud
ye shahr nikalta nahin jaadu ke asar se
is bhed ko allah ki qasam kah nahin sakte
apne hi jalve apne hi saae
phir lenge bose khanjar e qatil ke chaar panch
ek ummid se dil bahalta raha
is ka rakkhe kahan hisab koi
bigre hue mizaj ko pahchan jaiye
ham hain aur us ki yadgari hai
zabt ka hausla nahin baaqi
hansne siin us sajan ke tha shor mai kade men
gumnam jo hue hain vo lashkar talash kar
sunta nahin huun baat mukarrar kahe baghair
agar na ahd kiya ham ne ek hone ka
log kis vaste ghabrae hain
meri banhon men bahakne ki saza bhi sun le
shama ik qabr par jalani hai
pur hai saae ki tarah mera shabistan mujh se
vasim kitni hi subhen lahu lahu guzrin
vo shab o roz o mah o sal kahan
shairi zahr thi kya karen ai vasim
garmi sahi kalam men lekin na is qadar
aur tu hai ki sada aina khane mange
mere ghar us shokh ki do din se mehmani hui
tum apne daman men sitare baith kar tanko
kuchh balaen thiin asmani bhi
meri hi avaz men mujh ko gaane de
ishq ke maidan daron men bhi marne ka hai vasf bahut
ab kis ummid pe darvaze se jhanke koi
na gul khile hain na un se mile na mai pi hai
kisi charagh ke bas men dhuan nahin hota
ki buland ho ke bhi aadmi abhi khvahishon ka ghulam hai
zara si baat pe barpa qayamaten karni
ham rahi ki baat mat kar imtihan ho jaega
kabhi zindan men kabhi duur vatan se ai dost
ashiq ki be dili ka taghaful nahin javab
niklen ziyada daagh mire dil ke chaar panch
zindagi se yahi gila hai mujhe
gahre samundaron se jazire nikal kar
phirte liye hue ye musibat kahan kahan
titli ke paron ko kabhi chhilte nahin dekha
kise khabar hai ki duniya ka hashr kya hoga
ki jyuun phande men aate hain hiran ahista ahista
main tira tu mira javab hua
jo ab bhi teri gali se guzarne lagte hain
paani chhirke aag lagae
ajiib manzar hain hairat afza nazar jahan thi vahin thami hai
kuchh tujhe yaad dilane aae
urdu hai jis ka naam hamin jante hain dagh
tire do nain jab dekhun nazar bhar
munh lagate hi dagh itraya
ye mausam rim jhim ka mausam ye barkha ye mast faza
ye aur baat hai koi mahke koi chubhe
insan ki khvahishon ki koi intiha nahin
har haqiqat majaz ho jaae
tu hamara javab hai tanha
yaani main bhi nahin khuda bhi nahin
ibrat angez hai kya us ki javan margi bhi
ye bhi sach hai koi ulfat men pareshan kyuun ho
dil muddai o diida bana muddaa alaih
ik aap bala apne liye mol kharidi
haya se sar jhuka lena ada se muskura dena
nafs aur shara men larai aaj

dil sa dost na dil sa dushman
pak bazan e mohabbat men jo bebaki hai
ab nazar aata nahin kuchh bhi dukanon ke siva
jab ye karte hain pyaar ki baten
shirk chhora to sab ne chhor diya

vo chhalava is aan se nikla
ye sakht jan to qatl se nashad rah gaya

haq hai tera ye ashnaon par

jiit ke bhi vo sharminda hai haar ke bhi ham nazan
kuchh tabiat hi mili thi aisi chain se jiine ki surat na hui
hava men phirte ho kya hirs aur hava ke liye
hai ek tiir jis men donon chhide pare hain
lucknow jin se ibarat thi hue vo napadid
main raha umr bhar juda khud se

chandni raten bahar apni dikhati hain to kya
vo zindagi jo hasinon ke darmiyan guzre
gul bar kare haiga asbab e safar shayad
siyah bakhton ko yuun baagh se nikal ai charkh

na puchho mujh se lazzat khanaman barbad rahne ki

yaani jo kuchh bhi tha vo tha bhi nahin

sab puchhen the ahval jo koi dard ka maara guzre tha
kamar ki baat sunte hain ye kuchh paai nahin jaati
us ka lashkar jahan tahan yaani
nahin dariya to ho sarab koi
ankhon men dil khule hon to mausam ki qaid kya
chiriyon ki chahkar men gunje radha mohan ali ali
nanhe bachchon ne chhu bhi liya chand ko

kam nigahi son dekhte hain vale

phir bhi ai dost ghaur kar shayad
haan bhala kar tira bhala hoga
haal khulta nahin kuchh dil ke dharakne ka mujhe
shoale ugte nazar aae mujhe gulzar ke beach
ham ne kaabe men bhi iqrar kiya
lab tak aai na thi ki baat gai
tez andhi men to kheme bhi ukhar jaate hain

ujar ujar ke sanvarti hai tere hijr ki shaam

vo pahle apni zulfen to sambhalen

ab to ham chandni se rarte hain

ho raha hai jahan men andher

aisa bhi koi hai ki sab achchha kahen jise

ki meri khvab bandi ke liye hoga fusun vo bhi

ye kaun kahve hai abad kar hamen lekin
husn gar us ko samajh le to pasheman ho jaae
ab ye sochun to bhanvar zehn men par jaate hain
ham ko milta tira mizaj nahin
mira marna bhi to meri khushi se ho nahin sakta
kaam aata hai bure vaqt men aana tera
tiri yaad hai ya hai tera tasavvur
baat bigri thi kuchh aisi ki banai na gai
ye agar chahen to phir kya chahiye
ai saba kah bahar ki baten
mujh ko vo afsurdagi darkar hai
har ek se apni bhi tabiat nahin milti
chhup raha aa ke tire lab ke namak dan men aa
rahin adu se vahi garm joshiyan us ki
mujrim ho koi aur ho taqsir kisi ki
mir ke din o mazhab ko ab puchhte kya ho un ne to
main saal apne barha raha tha
aap se dil laga ke dekh liya
mujhe hai soch ye hi kis tarah hisab hua
mira ishq bhi kahani tira husn bhi fasana
ek raat aai ek raat gai
kisi ko shukr kisi ko shikayaten karni
naseh se koi kah de kiije kalam aisa
vo gul jis gulsitan men jalva farmai kare ghalib
kahan ham bhi rag o pai rakhte hain insaf bahattar hai
hamin pe un ki nazar hai hamin pe un ka karam
bekasi par meri apni tegh ki hasrat to dekh
maali ne aaj kitni duaen vasul kiin
yaani hamare jeb men ik taar bhi nahin
vo astin hai koi jo lahu na de nikle
phir har qadam pe ik nai divar dekhna
ham ne kaati hain tiri yaad men raten aksar
isa nafas huun aur salibon ke saath huun
paani pi pi ke tauba karta huun
ganva na mujh ko ki main tiri zindagi raha huun
jaan tujh par nisar kar baithe
ab safar ka istiara aur hai
zamin ki kaisi vakalat ho phir nahin chalti
ye tum ne kya kiya miri duniya men aa gae
dard ankhon se bahta hai aur chehra sab kuchh kahta hai
magar ye baat mohabbat ki baat par aai
kis jagah kis vaqt us ki khana virani hui
samne us ke kabhi us ki sataish nahin ki
nazara o khayal ka saman kiye hue
rah rau chale hai raah ko hamvar dekh kar
kya hoga rafugar se rafu mera gareban
ye bhi gulon ke saath pale hain bahar men
ham bhi mazmun ki hava bandhte hain
us ki sahar hai tu ki main us ki azaan hai tu ki main
menh na barsa aur ghata chhai bahut
ham mohabbat se mohabbat ka sila dete hain
munh ki baat sune har koi dil ke dard ko jaane kaun
har zarre ko gavah kiye ja raha huun main
saba ka narm sa jhonka bhi taziyana hua
jis ne paaya ho sar e rahguzar sannata
us ki mahfil men unhin ki raushni jin ke charagh
ye kis khalish ne phir is dil men ashiyana kiya
darvaza ghar ka khol ke phir ghar talash kar
hamin jab na honge to kya rang e mahfil
hai jo qismat men vahi hoga na kuchh kam na siva
jab guzarti hai us gali se saba
nae mahaul men ghabra rahe hain
vo atish huun jis men sharara nahin
haae kam bakht tu ne pi hi nahin
ishq ko be naqab hona tha
khuli hain ankhen magar badan hai tamam patthar
vo chamak ke barq rah gai vo garaj ke abr guzar gaya
vo gulon men gulon ki si bu na rahi vo azizon men lutf ki khu na rahi
vo shakhs tak mujhe tanha dikhai parta hai
ye sada kab ki suni aati hai phir kanon men
mujhe pamal raston ka safar achchha nahin lagta
gai le ke ruuh apni paigham dil ka
gharib dil ne bahut arzuen paida kiin
hamen dimagh kahan husn ke taqaza ka
maarifat khaliq ki aalam men bahut dushvar hai
bala ke pech men aaya hua hai
ham tahi dast tujh ko kya dete
ye bhi hai harf o hikayat mujhe maalum na tha
kitne jugnu isi khvahish men mire saath chale
jis tarah dil hua tukre az khud
isi junun men to barbad ghar hamare hue
khud ko main sab ankhon men
meri girah e dil na khuli hai na khulegi
hanuz tere tasavvur men hai nasheb o faraz
ye girah ab ke mire dil men pari ho jaise
vo pagal mast hai apni vafa men
hazar maujon ki ho kashakash magar ye dariya se paar hoga
dil ki derina rasm lagti hai
sitare jagte hain raat lat chhatkae soti hai
kaun mere siva akela tha
kai roz men aaj vo mehr liqa hua mere jo samne jalva numa
miri bala se bahar aae ya khizan guzre
janne vaale jaan lete hain
ye puchhe un se koi vo ghulam kis ka tha
khuda kare ki mile shaikh ko bhi ye taufiq
aap ke paanv phir kahan parte
hont julte hain mohabbat men dua se pahle
chhu ke dekha to garm tha matha
tu shahin hai parvaz hai kaam tera
vo bhi kya din the ki divana bane phirte the
magar main pahchanta tha jis ko vo aadmi ab kahin nahin hai
kaha ankhen koi talvon se mal raale agar kya ho
bana bana ke jo duniya mitai jaati hai
naseho us se nahin koi mafar ki surat
magar is ka hoga asar dekh lena
roya main is qadar ki mujhe aab le gaya
kya sitam hai ki ab tiri surat
ik musafir karvan dar karvan banta gaya
yuun ho ki aur hi koi hava mile mujhe
muddat se doston ki mahfil ko rhunrte hain

dil mat ganva khabar na sahi sair hi sahi
kiya tujh ishq ne aalam kuun majnun
hota hai shad kam yahan kaun ba zamir

ki rarte rarte khuda par bhi ashkar kiya

kis qadar aaina akela tha
sahab ko dil na dene pe kitna ghurur tha
tasvir khinch li hai tire shokh husn ki
ab aur kya kisi se marasim barhaen ham
aag ki zid pe na ja phir se bharak sakti hai
tiri vafa pe bhi kya kya hamen guman guzre
ye umr bhar jo pareshaniyan uthai hain ham ne
munh chhupana ham se chhora chahiye

har shai qarib aa ke kashish apni kho gai
achchhe bure ke farq ne basti ujaar di
hai yahi rasm to ye rasm utha di jaae
pighalte absharon ne hamen dariya dili di hai
guzarne ko to hazaron hi qafile guzre
aankh hi par dekhte hi teri surat khul gai
koi mahram nahin milta jahan men

miri taqat ki zamin thi buton ki naaz uthane ki

mir ab bahar aai sahra men chal junun kar
sab se magar aziiz hai teri nazar mujhe

rind ka saaqi ka mai ka khum ka paimane ka naam
jahan insan kho jaata ho khud phir us ki mahfil men
par teri anjuman ko bhi suna kiya na jaae
jo ye na sun sake ki zamana kharab hai

faiz itne vo kab hamare the

ye kis ki aarzu hai jo kaleje ko masalti hai

is tarah sar jhuka kar sail ko rhunrte hain
utha hi chahta hai koi tufan ham na kahte the
ghair az nigah ab koi haail nahin raha

kya bad guman hai mujh se ki aine men mire

aae ho kal aur aaj hi kahte ho ki jaun
bandagi hai ki ye khudai hai
jo ho kahna juda juda kahiye

apni hi sair karne ham jalva gar hue the

bu nahin aati ashnai ki
hai qais koi jo dil lagae

na uthe aah ka dhuan bhi ki vo

ye bojh agar dil se utar jaae to achchha

har ik kashti ka apna tajraba hota hai dariya men
mujhe bahana banana bhi to nahin aaya

raren hava se parinde khule paron vaale

hava chale na chale din palatte rahte hain

jon zakhm teri duuri men nasur ho gaya

usi ko kahte hain jannat usi ko dozakh bhi

tiri nigah ne fasane sunae hain kya kya
na khila jaane vo na kha jaane

abhi khush nava koi aur tha abhi par kusha koi aur hai
jo sunya tere dahan suun yak bachan
halka sa vo parda bhi divar nazar aaya
vo mujh se jiit bhi sakta tha jaane kyuun haara
phir koi aks shuaon se na bane paaya
be niyazi had se guzri banda parvar kab talak
hali utha hila ke mahfil ko
ham chand se aaj laut aae
ye naqsh dilon par se jaane ka nahin us ko
un hi logon ko muqabil men saf ara dekhna
baat sun li hai miri aaj khuda ne mere
rah gaya kaam hamara hi baghavat likhna
kuchh is ada se aaj vo pahlu nashin rahe
ranj ki jab guftugu hone lagi
raat ke haath pe ab koi diya chahta hai
har ik ki baat sunne par tavajjoh mat kar ai zalim
asman ne khush ho kar rang sa bikhera hai
bahut dinon se nahin hai kuchh us ki khair khabar
jis ko dil kahte the so tiir ka paikan nikla
ye gham kahan kahan ye masarrat kahan kahan
labon tak aae hue thartharae hain kya kya
rote hain to ham ko apne gham khane yaad aate hain
hairan hai jabin aaj kidhar sajda rava hai
qaid mehman hua jaata hai
shaam hue khush bash yahan ke mere paas aa jaate hain
tere dariya pe miri pyaas ka pahra hoga
us vaqt vo kuchh aur bhi aate hain siva yaad
jitne the hadsat mujhe raas aa gae
kya taajjub hai jo us ko dekh kar aa jaae rahm
sab shama ko jalate hain sanche men rhaal ke
kyuun miri shakl pahan leta hai chhupne ke liye
vaae nakami ki us kafir ka khanjar tez hai
mol mera makan lete hain
shakh to sirf dua karti hai
akhir ko kitni der se ghar jaana chahiye
tere rukh ki naqab hain ham log
hasti apni habab ki si hai
aalam men ab o gil ke kyunkar nibah hoga
us men majnun ka magar matam raha
zulf teri hai mauj jamuna ki
koi to harf lab e charagar se nikla tha
vaqt khvabon men ganvaya hai bahut din ham ne
misal is ki kahan hai koi zamane men
ye raqam na haath lagti na ye iftikhar hota
ye dil nahin hai bagula uchhal raha hai miyan
qasid ke aate aate khat ik aur likh rakhun
ye kab kaha tha ki vo khush badan hamara ho
ham to dushman ko dost kar lete
gham ka putla bana diya tu ne
kya ilaaj us ka agar ho muddaa un ka yahi
jo mere haal ki gardish kuun dekhe
laakh pardon men chhupa koi sanvarta hi raha
ye aur baat ki bachne ke ghar nahin aate
duniya nazar aai mujhe to kya nazar aaya
ye ilm ka sauda ye risale ye kitaben
tere paimane men gardish nahin baaqi saaqi
gulshan gulshan phuul
aankh khulti hai to sab khvab bikhar jaate hain
bahut dinon baad paai fursat to main ne khud ko palat ke dekha
ye qurb kya hai ki yak jan hue na duur rahe
ye ham jo hijr men divar o dar ko dekhte hain
zikr har subh o shaam hai tera
pardagi ham se kyuun rakha parda
havas nasib nazar ko kahin qarar nahin
kuchh hamari khabar nahin aati
sitam ishq men aap asan na samjhen
saare buute pata pata ravish ravish barbad hue
mujh ko sambhal had se guzarne laga huun main
kaun sa dil hai jis men dagh nahin
dil se tiri nigah jigar tak utar gai
is dasht men ik shahr tha vo kya hua avargi
har ik ko hai guman ki mukhatib hamin rahe
meri khatir kyuun uljhe ho
use jakra hua tha zindagi ne
nigahen churane ke din aa rahe hain
jis be dil be tab o tavan ko ishq ka maara jaane hai
gar maze ka gila kare koi
partav se aftab ke zarre men jaan hai
ham apni aag men har roz jal ke dekhte hain
us mathe ko chume kitne din biite
bhage the ham bahut so usi ki saza hai ye
kuchh aaj shaam se chehra hai faq sahar ki tarah
is se hai gharibon ko tasalli ki ajal ne
pahle hargiz na jaan se nikla
kab hai inkar tire lutf o karam se lekin
chhor kar jis ko gae the aap koi aur tha
ab bhi tum meri zindagi ho kya
jaali ki kurti hai vo bala gote ki dhanak phir vaisi hi
supurdagi ke taqaze kahan kahan se parhun
tasbih ke saath us ne to zunnar bhi chhora
jab apni raah chalta hai akela hone lagta hai
ki bin kahe hi unhen sab khabar hai kya kahiye
nazar men sabhon ki khuda kar chale
go bhalai karke ham jinson se khush hota hai ji
sharabi jama hain mai khana men topi uchhalti hai
us ka kuchh maqsud koi muddaa ho ya na ho
karte ho dagh duur se but khane ko salam
ankhon men hai vo qatra ki gauhar na hua tha
muhaba kya hai main zamin idhar dekh
ab vo saaqi ki bhi ankhen na rahin rindon men
sanam ki zulfan ko hijr men ab gae hain mujh nain hain khvab rahat
kya qasam hai tire milne ki ki kha bhi na sakun
koi dil dikhae agar dekh lena
haqiqat men jo rahzan hai vahi rahbar hai khatre men
jabr par sabr kiya karte hain
samajhne vaale samajhte hain pech ki taqrir
dil mira hai usi nagar ki taraf
hamare shauq ki ye intiha thi
har mahine men jo ye badr se hota hai hilal
yahan aa ke jo kuchh suna tha na dekha
yaani tire firaq men khuub sharab pi gai
pinhan tha daam sakht qarib ashiyan ke
vaan irada aaj us qatil ke dil men aur hai
na yagane hain na begane tiri mahfil men
vafa dari ka daava kyuun karen ham
zindagi roz to tasvir banane se rahi
har aadmi ke muqaddar men ghar nahin hota
un ko khalvat sara men be parda
ankhon ko rakh ke taaq pe dekha kare koi
narkh tu kya puchhta hai ab to qimat khul gai
furqat ho ya visal vahi iztirab hai
us ki ankhon ki nim khvabi se
har sada par bulati rahi raat bhar
kyuun ki ye baaz ashyani hai
tum apna ranj o gham apni pareshani mujhe de do
jo ghair the vo isi baat par hamare hue
tu ne kis ghari zalim meri ham navai ki
jaane na jaane gul hi na jaane baagh to saara jaane hai
yaar laae miri balin pe use par kis vaqt
idhar to aa mire khush rang pattharon vaale
ye kaisi zid hai ki dariya kisi se paar na ho
tiri nigah ne pahlu bachae hain kya kya
hava khilaf ho maujon pe ikhtiyar na ho
khazana tum na paae to gharib jaise ho gae
main usi tarah to bahalta huun
duniya hai chal chalav ka rasta sambhal ke chal
siine men na ik aag si bharkaen to soen
de dete hain ham kar ke raqam kah nahin sakte
tira gham kya hai bas ye janta huun
dhundli si ek yaad kisi qabr ka diya
ek se jaane ham kitne ho jaate hain
bahut dinon men taghaful ne tere paida ki
aadmi ko bhi mayassar nahin insan hona
ja raha huun jis taraf le ja raha hai dil mujhe
dekhiye aaj faqiron ka kidhar phera hai
ek muddat se tamanna thi tumhen chhune ki
khuli hoti hain go ankhen magar bina nahin hotin
hai kuchh aisa ki us ki jalvat men
hain zaval amada ajza afrinash ke tamam
is men teri zaban lete hain
ho jaae jhuut sach yahi khubi bayan ki hai
hava ki raah men ye shama jal to sakti hai
ki main to khud apne ghar men bhi do ghari raha huun
ai vali kyuun sun sake naseh ki baat
mahmil men baithe hain aur mahmil ko rhunrte hain
maze ishq ke kuchh vahi jante hain
tu na cheta yaan bahut din kam raha
subuk sar ban ke kya puchhen ki ham se sargiran kyuun ho
ye mera gaanv to pahchanta lage hai mujhe
khuda jaane kya kya khayal aa rahe hain
vahi maasum hai har baat pe jo sad kare
basti men jitne ab gazida the sab ke sab
mazhabi bahas main ne ki hi nahin
ye be avaz banjar ban ke baasi ye udasi
aap bhi kuchh kahte kahte rah jaate hain
mir ham mil ke bahut khush hue tum se pyare
tu aaj ka shair hai to kar meri tarah baat
ham apne aap ko ik masala bana na sake
pak bazan men hai mashhur vali
per ki chal se ragar kha kar
ye jahan ajab jahan hai na qafas na ashiyana
jagne vaalo ta ba sahar khamosh raho
kuchh na kuchh raah pe le aae hain
rang khulta jaae hai jitna ki urta jaae hai
jis din vo milne aai hai us din ki rudad ye hai
ishq ki aabru rubo baithe
bekasi meri sharik aina tera ashna
be gunahi ke libade ko utaro bhi nasir
bijli ke taar pe baitha hua hansta panchhi
us fitna khu ke dar se ab uthte nahin asad
ham se puchh kar dekho raaz parda daron ke
ulta mujhi pe rakhte hain ehsan lijiye
agar gul sarv ke qamat pe pairahan na ho jaave
ik pal ke jhapakne tak har khel suhana hai
dil dharakta hai safar ke hangam
phir bhi kisi shumar men huun bhi nahin bhi huun
har baar hava na hogi dar par
chale to us ko zamane thahar ke dekhte hain
chahiye us ka tasavvur hi se naqsha khinchna
teri samjhaute baz duniya men
puchha akbar hai aadmi kaisa
rang har rang men hai dad talab
chali hai kaisi hava ilahi ki har tabiat men barhami hai
allah ke sheron ko aati nahin rubahi
vo mire dil men samae hain ki ji janta hai
ab samajhne laga huun sud o ziyan
ai falak kah to kya irada hai
matlab ki kah rahe hain vo daana hamin to hain
haq men logon ke hamari to hai aadat likhna
ye bhi kya kam hai ki donon ka bharam qaaem hai
dast bardar agar aap ghazab se ho jaaen
ab kya kahun jaati hai miri aah kahan tak
shikva to ek chher hai lekin haqiqatan
aur barha di shauq ki uljhan

aur sitam ye ki mera ji hi nahin
ik main hi is dayar men huun bhi nahin bhi huun

achchha nahin chehre par lohu ka baha jaana
kitna asan tha tire hijr men marna janan
raat din teri intizari hai

akhlaq men jhuut bolta huun
us baav ne hamen to diya sa bujha diya

ban tum ab bhi bah rahi ho kya
kab jaan lahu hogi kab ashk guhar hoga
ham aur tabibon ki dava kuchh nahin karte
yaad aata hai hamen haae zamana dil ka

dil ne ik naala kiya aaj is tarah divana var

aage bichha ke nata ko laate the tegh o tasht
ishq men la javab hain ham log
haal is ka kis se puchha chahiye
allah allah ke siva akhir raha kuchh bhi na yaad
ghazlon ki dhanak orh mire shoala badan tu
kisht hai sarsabz aur nichi hai baad
mire sukut men kya kya khalal raha hai miyan
sur se sanson ka naata hai torun kaise
roz lete hain naya dil dilbar
ya rahebi kar ya padshahi
khuda ke haath men mat saunp saare kamon ko

ye vaqt jo tera hai ye vaqt jo mera hai
na muvafiq thi zamane ki hava mere liye
har lamha mujh men banta bikharta hua sa kuchh
bahut dil ko kushada kar liya kya
aur ham ne to baat bhi ki hai
dil ne chaha bhi agar honton ne jumbish nahin ki
be kaif kuchh afaq ki aaza shikani se
aati hai ham ko sharm ki kya munh dikhaen ham
vo vahima ki jis se tera yaqin aaya
de aur dil un ko jo na de mujh ko zaban aur
zaruri ho gai hai dil ki zinat
us nagri men qadam qadam pe sar ko jhukana parta hai
ab junun aap hai gareban gir
ham ye to nahin kahte ki gham kah nahin sakte
bedar kar ke tere badan ki khud agahi
tanha na vali jag munin likhta hai tire vasf
us ke shaitan ko kahan taufiq
dene ke ai karim magar hain hazar haath
koshish to ki sabhi ne magar ban pare ki baat
ik zara sher kahun aur main kya kya dekhun
ho ke nadim jurm se phir jurm karte hain vahi
ishq par zor nahin hai ye vo atish ghalib
ho yuun ki aur hi koi aadam mile tumhen
kaun rota hai kisi aur ki khatir ai dost
ai khuda jo kahin nahin maujud

gae ho jab se yahan subh o shaam hi na hui
kuchh taalluq raha na duniya se

be ishare bhi kuchh ishare hain

liya danton men jo tinka hua resha niyastan ka

roz ik chiiz tuut jaati hai
kya haal hai kya dikha rahe ho

nadida ik nigah kiye ja raha huun main

kitna bara mazaq hua raushni ke saath
samundar hai adakari kare hai
agar hota vo dushman ki kaman se
phir khuda jaane ye hangama kahan tak pahunche

angraiyon men phailte hain baar baar haath

hasti hamari apni fana par dalil hai
na ham qatra samajhte hain na ham dariya samajhte hain
ishq ka shikva e beja bhi na be kar gaya
nilgun shabnami kapron men badan ki ye jot
mila hai kya use kaliyon ko be numu kar ke

ki jyuun aahu kuun karte hain shikar ahista ahista
thahar thahar ke jala dil ko ek baar na phunk
aur is par tumhen aata hai jalana dil ka
haan mujhe chandni se khatra hai
nazuk si nigahon men nazuk sa fasana hai
asl maqsud ka har chiiz men milta hai pata
kisi navvab ne na raaja ne

ya gai kuchh barh shakebai bahut

vaadi bahut muhib hai bim o umid ka

dekhiye vo kya karen ham kya karen
ye palkon par nae aansu nahin hain
alava iid ke milti hai aur din bhi sharab
kahte the jo ab koi nahin jaan se guzarta
dil se ik khauf sa guzra hai bichhar jaane ka
vo jo na aane vaala hai na us se mujh ko matlab tha

dete hain dil ki kharabi ki khabar donon saath
pa ba gil sab hain rihai ki kare tadbir kaun
dekha jo main ne yaar to vo mir hi nahin
vaise to ik aansu hi baha kar mujhe le jaae
so ham bhi us ki gali se guzar ke dekhte hain
lekin kabhi kabhi ise tanha bhi chhor de
nahin ki mujh ko qayamat ka etiqad nahin
tamasha kishvar aina men aina band aaya
ashna ke naam se pyara hai begane ka naam
maraz men mubtala kar ke marizon ko dava dena
main teri zaat men gum ho saka na tu mujh men
tegh zan dashna guzar o navak afgan aap hain
vo mere sab havale janta hai
kuchh nishan umr ki raftar se lag jaate hain
chashm o dil bina hai apne roz o shab ai marduman
dil ko kai kahaniyan yaad si aa ke rah gaiin
dil laga kar lag gaya un ko bhi tanha baithna
magar ye shauharon se apne be parva nahin hotin
kuchh aur chahiye vusat mire bayan ke liye
ye husn ye shokhi ye karishma ye adaen

apni iza dahi ko bhuul gaya

koi dilchasp muraqqa na dikhana hargiz

abhi zinda huun lekin sochta rahta huun khalvat men

koi roti jo khara maang raha hai mujh se

alfaz parakhta rahta hai

aankh jhapki hi thi ki raat gai
tu fath mand mera tarasha hua sanam
koi ye puchhe ki vaaiz ka kya bigarta hai
us ko taqaza rava mujh pe taqaza haram
aur kya is se ziyada koi narmi bartun

us ke giton se zamane ko sanvaren yaaro
un se pyaar kiya hai ham ne un ki raah men ham baithe hain

ye dil ka daagh jo khud bhi bhala lage hai mujhe
